EARS requirements

Every requirement from docs/requirements/, one file per area, the source of truth. Implemented in lists the classes whose documentation cites the ID; Verified by lists the tests tagged with it, the same tags the build's traceability check reads. 559 of 559 live requirements have a tagged test. The readings behind the numbers are in Requirement readings.

Core Resilience Release Waves Radar Memory Adapt Dials Distance Power Endgame Movement Time budget Bullet shielding Melee (2.1) Posture gate Melee radar Melee sensing Melee movement Melee gun Melee memory

Core

CORE-1UbiquitousS1

The core shall not import any class from robocode.*.

Implemented in
HadurCore, adapt (package), gun (package), knn (package), ledger (package), melee (package), BotEvent, model (package), move (package), core (package), Angles, Rules, physics (package), policy (package), port (package), replay (package), role (package), hadur117 (package)
Verified by
ArchitectureTest.java, PhysicsMatchesEngineProperties.java, core.feature
CORE-2UbiquitousS1

The core shall produce identical BotOrders for identical BotInput sequences and identical profile state.

Implemented in
Bench, Hadur, HadurRecorder, HadurCore, adapt (package), SampledGun, gun (package), KdTree, knn (package), LinkCodec, TeamDodge, ProfileLibrary, memory (package), BotEvent, BotInput, BotOrders, RobotStateLog, model (package), move (package), core (package), MovementPredictor, physics (package), TickBudget, MemoryProfileStore, LineCodec, Replay, replay (package), AimJitter, shield (package), ModalOffset, ShieldMode, shieldmode (package), hadur117 (package)
Verified by
ArchitectureTest.java, CoreMemoryTest.java, LineCodecProperties.java, ReplayTest.java, core.feature

Resilience

RES-1UnwantedS1

If the core throws on any tick, then the adapter shall issue the safe order set for that tick and record the fault.

Implemented in
Hadur, Guard, HadurCore, DuelController, BotOrders, RoundStats, MoveController, core (package), Telemetry, Replay, Role, hadur117 (package)
Verified by
GuardTest.java, resilience.feature
RES-2UbiquitousS1

The core shall bound every data structure that grows during a battle.

Implemented in
HadurCore, MeleeMemory, AntiSurferGun, GunController, HybridGun, MainGun, TripHammerGun, gun (package), KdTree, KnnView, knn (package), EnemyHistory, MeleeProfileFolder, MeleeWaves, OpponentStatsBook, OpponentProfile, ProfileCodec, ProfileFolder, memory (package), RobotStateLog, RoundStats, WaveManager, model (package), BulletShadows, MoveController, core (package), BattleHitRates, EnergyRace, HitWindow, policy (package), RoleResolver, ShieldDetector, ModalOffset, ShieldList, ShieldMode, shieldmode (package), EnemyInfo, EnemyTracker
Verified by
OpponentStatsTest.java, ProfileFolderTest.java, ResourceBoundsTest.java, HitWindowTest.java, RoleResolverTest.java, EnemyShotTest.java, EnemyTrackerBoundsTest.java, resilience.feature
RES-3UbiquitousS3

The store shall write a profile to a temporary file before replacing it, and remove the temporary file only once the profile is complete, so that an interrupted write leaves the previous profile, or the complete new one, loadable.

Implemented in
FileProfileStore, HadurCore, MeleeMemory, ProfileCodec, ProfileLibrary, memory (package), core (package), MemoryProfileStore, ProfileStore, port (package), hadur117 (package)
Verified by
ProfileLibraryTest.java, memory.feature, FileProfileStoreTest.java
RES-4UnwantedS4

If the live hit-rate estimate diverges from the profile's by more than the margin of error, then the core shall decay the seed weight to zero within 20 waves.

Implemented in
Report, HadurCore, Opening, SeedTrust, adapt (package), DuelController, AntiSurferGun, GunController, MainGun, SampledGun, gun (package), KnnView, knn (package), Estimate, LineageKey, RoundStats, SeedWeight, model (package), MoveController, move (package), core (package)
Verified by
SeedTrustProperties.java, SeedTrustTest.java, GunOpeningTest.java, SurfOpeningTest.java, adapt.feature
RES-5UbiquitousS1

Every degradation and fault counter shall be included in the round statistics and the bench report.

Implemented in
BattleResult, LogHarvester, Report, Hadur, Guard, HadurCore, MeleeSeam, DuelController, ProfileLibrary, RoundStats, core (package), Telemetry
Verified by
TelemetryReportTest.java, CoreMemoryTest.java, RoundStatsTest.java, resilience.feature
RES-6UbiquitousS1

The core shall contain no use of unseeded randomness, threads, reflection, or file I/O.

Implemented in
Hadur, HadurCore, adapt (package), DuelController, SampledGun, KdTree, knn (package), ledger (package), LinkCodec, link (package), MeleeProfileCodec, melee (package), Bytes, ProfileLibrary, memory (package), BotEvent, model (package), MirrorDrive, move (package), core (package), physics (package), TickBudget, policy (package), MemoryProfileStore, ProfileStore, Telemetry, port (package), LineCodec, replay (package), role (package), AimJitter, shield (package), ShieldList, ShieldMode, shieldmode (package)
Verified by
ArchitectureTest.java, MinimumRiskMovementProperties.java, MinimumRiskMovementTest.java, resilience.feature
RES-7UnwantedR4

If the core has faulted on three ticks of a round off a team (WEAVE-5), then the guard's safe orders shall also fire power 1.0 at the enemy's last scanned bearing whenever the gun is cool.

Implemented in
Guard
Verified by
GuardTest.java
RES-8EventR4

When a battle ends, the adapter shall write a battle-health record of at most 64 bytes with rounds, rounds survived, faults, skipped turns, memory failures and the learned tick allowance.

Implemented in
Hadur, HadurRecorder, HadurCore, DuelController
Verified by
BattleHealthTest.java
RES-10UbiquitousR7

The robot shall leave no reference to its classes in the process after a battle, so that the number of classes loaded in one JVM stays flat across a session of battles.

Implemented in
no citation in the sources
Verified by
RobotLoaderTest.java
RES-15EventR9

When a battle ends, the health record shall add the setup time, the first turn the main loop ran and the longest run of consecutive skipped turns, and stay within 64 bytes.

Implemented in
Hadur, HadurRecorder, HadurCore
Verified by
BattleHealthTest.java

Release

REL-1UbiquitousS2

The robot jar shall contain only class files that a Java 11 runtime can load, so that every RoboRumble client can run it.

Implemented in
BotEvent, hadur117 (package)
Verified by
ReleaseTargetTest.java

Waves

WAVE-1EventS2

When an enemy energy drop is observed, the core shall subtract damage dealt by our bullets, enemy wall damage and enemy hit refunds before classifying it as a fired bullet.

Implemented in
BattleResult, LogHarvester, Report, Hadur, DuelSeam, DuelController, EnergyLedger, ledger (package), ProfileFolder, BotEvent, RoundStats, Wave, WaveManager, MoveController, core (package), Rules, physics (package)
Verified by
WaveMatcherTest.java, EnergyLedgerProperties.java, EnergyLedgerTest.java, waves.feature
WAVE-2UnwantedS2

If a corrected energy drop is outside [0.1, 3.0], then the core shall not create a firing wave.

Implemented in
DuelController, EnergyLedger, ledger (package), BotEvent, RoundStats, Wave, WaveManager, MoveController, core (package), Rules
Verified by
EnergyLedgerProperties.java, EnergyLedgerTest.java, waves.feature
WAVE-3UnwantedR2

If a shot is found on the same interval as an inferred wall hit, then the movement wave it becomes shall be marked uncertain and surfed at half weight.

Implemented in
ShadowAvoidance, EnergyLedger, Wave, AesRecord, MoveController, SurfMover
Verified by
EnergyLedgerTest.java, FiringWaveTest.java
WAVE-4EventR6

When a movement wave's bullet power is updated, its wall distances shall be recomputed.

Implemented in
Wave, MoveController
Verified by
DangerIntegrationTest.java
WAVE-5EventR9

When our own energy falls by exactly 0.1 on a tick of a duel and no shot of ours, bullet hit, wall hit or robot hit explains it, the core shall take 0.1 off the enemy's energy drop for that tick (the engine's inactivity drain) before classifying the drop as a fired bullet, for every such tick since the last scan.

Implemented in
HadurCore, DuelController, EnergyLedger, Rules
Verified by
EnergyLedgerTest.java

Radar

RADAR-1UnwantedS2

If no scan of the enemy arrived on the previous tick, then the core shall turn the radar toward the enemy's last known bearing until it scans the enemy again.

Implemented in
BattleResult, LogHarvester, Report, DuelController, BotOrders, RoundStats, core (package)
Verified by
RadarReacquireTest.java, waves.feature

Memory

MEM-1EventS3

When the first scan of a battle names an opponent, the core shall load the profile for that opponent's lineage key before producing orders for that tick.

Implemented in
Report, adapt (package), DuelController, LineageKey, OpponentProfile, ProfileLibrary, memory (package), core (package), hadur117 (package)
Verified by
CoreMemoryTest.java, LineageKeyProperties.java, ProfileLibraryTest.java, memory.feature
MEM-2EventS3

When a round ends, the core shall fold that round's gun, movement and outcome statistics into the opponent profile.

Implemented in
HadurCore, DuelController, GunController, OpponentProfile, ProfileFolder, memory (package)
Verified by
CoreMemoryTest.java, ProfileFolderTest.java, memory.feature
MEM-3EventS3

When a battle ends, the store shall persist the profile atomically within the data quota.

Implemented in
Bench, Report, Hadur, HadurCore, DuelController, ProfileLibrary, memory (package), RoundStats, port (package), hadur117 (package)
Verified by
ArchitectureTest.java, CoreMemoryTest.java, ProfileCodecProperties.java, ProfileLibraryTest.java, memory.feature, FileProfileStoreTest.java
MEM-4UnwantedS3

If a profile fails to load or fails its checksum, then the core shall proceed with an empty profile and record the failure.

Implemented in
LogHarvester, Report, FileProfileStore, Hadur, MeleeMemory, DuelController, Bytes, ProfileCodec, ProfileFormatException, ProfileLibrary, memory (package), RoundStats, ProfileStore, port (package)
Verified by
CoreMemoryTest.java, ProfileCodecProperties.java, ProfileCodecTest.java, ProfileLibraryTest.java, memory.feature
MEM-5StateS3

While the store is above 90% of quota, the store shall evict gun and surf seeds from the least-recently-fought profiles before writing.

Implemented in
Report, FileProfileStore, HadurCore, DuelController, OpponentProfile, ProfileLibrary, memory (package), RoundStats, core (package), ProfileStore, port (package)
Verified by
ProfileCodecTest.java, ProfileLibraryTest.java, memory.feature
MEM-6UbiquitousR1

The store shall keep statistics loadable at quota by evicting seeds from every profile before any statistics are skipped.

Implemented in
ProfileLibrary
Verified by
ProfileLibraryTest.java
MEM-7UbiquitousR1

The profile codec shall be able to write a profile in the format a previous release can read.

Implemented in
ProfileCodec, ProfileLibrary
Verified by
ProfileCodecTest.java, ProfileLibraryTest.java
MEM-8UbiquitousR5

The store shall keep seeds only for at most five opponents met at least twice with a recorded score share under 60%, and shall keep every other profile as statistics of at most 1 KB.

Implemented in
HadurCore, OpponentProfile, ProfileLibrary
Verified by
ProfileLibraryTest.java
MEM-9UbiquitousR5

The store shall keep its files under a subdirectory named for the profile-format version and shall not read or write another version's subdirectory except to carry tiers forward once.

Implemented in
ProfileCodec, ProfileLibrary
Verified by
ProfileLibraryTest.java
MEM-10UbiquitousR5

The adapter shall do at most one profile read and two profile writes per battle, each at most 2 KB, apart from MEM-8's seeded profiles.

Implemented in
HadurCore, DuelController, ProfileLibrary, Role
Verified by
CoreMemoryTest.java
MEM-11UbiquitousR8

The adapter shall list the data directory at most once per battle and answer every later name, size and age question from an index it keeps current.

Implemented in
FileProfileStore, ProfileLibrary, ProfileStore
Verified by
ProfileLibraryTest.java, FileProfileStoreTest.java
MEM-12UbiquitousR8

The library shall read a stored profile to look for seeds only when its file is larger than 1 KB.

Implemented in
ProfileLibrary
Verified by
ProfileLibraryTest.java
MEM-13UnwantedR8

If a save would take the store past 90% of its quota after every other seed is gone, then the library shall delete the least recently written profiles until it fits, and write.

Implemented in
FileProfileStore, ProfileLibrary, MemoryProfileStore, ProfileStore
Verified by
ProfileLibraryTest.java
MEM-15EventR9

When a battle starts, the robot's store shall build its index from one saved index file and the directory's names, asking no file its size or age, unless the saved index is damaged or was written for other names.

Implemented in
FileProfileStore
Verified by
FileProfileStoreTest.java

Adapt

ADAPT-1StateS4

While the loaded profile's movement tier is M2 or M3, the gun shall use the anti-surfer aim from the first firing wave.

Implemented in
Report, Opening, OpeningBook, adapt (package), DuelController, AntiSurferGun, GunController, gun (package), Tiers, core (package)
Verified by
OpeningBookTest.java, GunOpeningTest.java, CoreMemoryTest.java, adapt.feature

While the loaded profile's gun tier is T3, movement shall enable the flattener views from the first surfable wave.

Implemented in
HadurCore, Opening, OpeningBook, adapt (package), DuelController, Tiers, FlattenerFormula, MoveController, move (package)
Verified by
OpeningBookTest.java, SurfOpeningTest.java, adapt.feature
ADAPT-3UbiquitousS4

The core shall weight seeded samples lower than samples observed in the current battle.

Implemented in
Report, HadurCore, Opening, OpeningBook, SeedLoader, adapt (package), DuelController, AntiSurferGun, GunController, MainGun, gun (package), KnnView, knn (package), ProfileLibrary, Seeds, SeedWeight, Timestamped, TimestampedFiringAngle, TimestampedGuessFactor, model (package), FlattenerFormula, MoveController, MoveFormula, move (package), core (package)

ADAPT-4EventR5

When a profile with a known gun tier and no seeds loads, the core shall apply the opening book's choices from the first tick without replaying samples.

Implemented in
no citation in the sources
Verified by
OpeningBookTest.java
ADAPT-5EventD1

When a profile that records POW-7's hit-rate condition as met loads, the core shall apply POW-7 to POW-9 from the first shot, until this battle's rates contradict it by more than their margins.

Implemented in
Opening, OpeningBook, DuelController, OpponentProfile, ProfileCodec, ProfileFolder, PowerPolicy

Dials

DIAL-1UbiquitousS4

Every policy input shall carry a value and a margin of error, and each policy shall select its conservative setting while the margin exceeds the policy's threshold.

Implemented in
HadurCore, Opening, OpeningBook, adapt (package), DuelController, GunController, gun (package), KnnView, Estimate, Tiers, memory (package), MoveController, SimpleFormula, move (package), core (package), DistancePolicy, HitWindow, MoveFlavour, PowerPolicy, policy (package)
Verified by
OpeningBookTest.java, CoreMemoryTest.java, EstimateTest.java, TiersTest.java, SurfOpeningTest.java, DistancePolicyProperties.java, DistancePolicyTest.java, HitWindowTest.java, MoveFlavourTest.java, PowerPolicyTest.java, adapt.feature, aggressive.feature, unhittable.feature
DIAL-2UbiquitousS4

The core shall not condition any policy on elapsed ticks or round number alone.

Implemented in
OpeningBook, SeedTrust, adapt (package), DuelController, BotEvent, BotInput, SeedWeight, core (package), BattleHitRates, Endgame, EscapeTrial, Matchup, MirrorDetector, TickBudget, policy (package)
Verified by
OpeningBookTest.java, ArchitectureTest.java, TickBudgetTest.java, adapt.feature
DIAL-3UnwantedR1

If a margin used in a policy comparison is not a finite number, then the policy shall take its conservative branch rather than treat the comparison as settled.

Implemented in
SeedTrust
Verified by
EstimateTest.java

Distance

DIST-1StateS5

While our rolling hit rate exceeds the enemy's by 5 points or more, the distance policy shall reduce the target distance by 25 px per wave, not below 400 px.

Implemented in
Report, ShieldTableData, HadurCore, Opening, OpeningBook, DuelController, Estimate, RoundStats, MoveController, SurfMover, move (package), core (package), DistancePolicy, HitWindow, policy (package)
Verified by
TelemetryReportTest.java, OpeningBookTest.java, DistancePolicyProperties.java, DistancePolicyTest.java, HitWindowTest.java, aggressive.feature
DIST-4OptionalR9

Where the mode table's dist-pin: <px> <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, the surf's desired distance shall be the pinned px (held to 150-900) instead of DIST-1's controller target, except while END-1 is finishing. It copies the distance band Saguaro or BeepBoop holds against that robot (from truth logs). The list is ShieldTableData.distPins(): Ololobot pinned at 625 px held +3.2 on 12 fresh PC seeds (pc-locks round 4, PR #866) (build-ablation.sh dplist); tested by ShieldListTest.perOpponentDistPin.

Implemented in
Hadur, ShieldTableData, DuelController, ShieldList
Verified by
ShieldListTest.java

Power

POW-1OptionalS5

Where the profile's gun tier is T0 and enemy energy exceeds 12, the gun shall fire power 3.0.

Implemented in
Report, ShieldTableData, DuelController, GunController, Tiers, RoundStats, core (package), PowerPolicy, policy (package), ShieldList
Verified by
PowerPolicyTest.java, aggressive.feature
POW-2StateS5

While our rolling hit rate exceeds 20% and the enemy's is below 10%, each by more than its margin of error, the gun shall fire power 3.0, capped at a quarter of the enemy's energy.

Implemented in
Report, HadurCore, DuelController, GunController, Estimate, RoundStats, core (package), HitWindow, PowerPolicy, policy (package)
Verified by
PowerPolicyTest.java, aggressive.feature
POW-3StateR2

While the profile rates their gun T1, they are within 450 px and their energy exceeds 12, the gun shall fire power 3.0.

Implemented in
DuelController, PowerPolicy
Verified by
PowerPolicyTest.java
POW-4StateR2

While no full-power rule applies and both our and their rolling hit rates are known within 5 points, the gun shall fire whichever of its own power or 3.0 gives the higher expected value (hit rate times bullet damage, less the power spent), never below its own choice.

Implemented in
ShieldTableData, DuelController, PowerPolicy, ShieldList
Verified by
PowerPolicyTest.java
POW-5StateR3

While the enemy's gun tier is T3 and their distance exceeds 500 px, the gun shall fire no more than 1.7 unless a full-power rule applies.

Implemented in
DuelController, PowerPolicy
Verified by
PowerAffordabilityTest.java, PowerPolicyTest.java
POW-7StateD1

While both robots' battle-long hit rates over all their bullets are below 12.5% by more than their margins, and our energy is no more than 3 below the enemy's, the gun shall fire power 0.1.

Implemented in
Opening, OpeningBook, DuelController, GunController, OpponentProfile, ProfileCodec, ProfileFolder, BattleHitRates, PowerPolicy
Verified by
GunStakesTest.java, LeadAwarePowerProperties.java, LeadAwarePowerTest.java, druss.feature
POW-8StateD1

While POW-7's hit-rate condition holds, our energy is more than 3 below the enemy's and our energy exceeds 10, the gun shall fire its default power.

Implemented in
PowerPolicy
Verified by
GunStakesTest.java, LeadAwarePowerProperties.java, LeadAwarePowerTest.java, druss.feature
POW-9StateD1

While both robots' energy exceeds 60, the gun shall fire its default power whatever POW-7 would choose.

Implemented in
DuelController, GunController, PowerPolicy
Verified by
LeadAwarePowerProperties.java, LeadAwarePowerTest.java, druss.feature
POW-10UbiquitousD1

The gun shall fire every duel shot that our energy can pay for, lowering the power where a rule saves energy, except where END-4 holds the shot.

Implemented in
DuelController, PowerPolicy
Verified by
PowerAffordabilityTest.java, LeadAwarePowerProperties.java, LeadAwarePowerTest.java, druss.feature
POW-11UbiquitousD1

The core shall keep battle-long hit rates for both robots in three bullet power classes (under 0.2, 0.2 to 1.2, above 1.2) and over all their bullets, each with its margin of error.

Implemented in
HadurCore, DuelController, GunController, RoundStats, MoveController, BattleHitRates, Matchup
Verified by
RoundStatsTest.java, BattleHitRatesProperties.java, BattleHitRatesTest.java, druss.feature
POW-13UbiquitousM7

In a duel, the gun's base power shall be 2.95 inside 150 px and 1.95 beyond, under the same caps. (The close200 arm, power 3 inside 200 px, failed and is removed.)

Implemented in
GunController
Verified by
ClosePowerTest.java
POW-15OptionalR9

Where the mode table's full-off: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, the shot shall keep the gun's own power where a full-power rule (POW-1 to POW-4) would have raised it, and a P record full-off shall say so at the battle's first scan; every other rule after the gun's choice applies unchanged. The list is ShieldTableData.fullOffs() (robots added by cards, the pc-tailor-field picks among them (BENCH-197, PR #686); ablation fplist from $FULL_OFF, suffix fp).

Implemented in
Hadur, ShieldTableData, DuelController, ShieldList
Verified by
ShieldListTest.java
POW-16OptionalR9

Where the mode table's x5-power: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, each shot shall leave at the highest x.x5 power at or below the power chosen (at most four, 0.1 apart, each below our energy) whose speed read back from our energy drop, energy - (energy - p), rounds to another tenth by Math.round(speed * 10) than the power's own speed, and at the chosen power when none does or it is below 0.15; the wave carries the power fired. A surfer that matches the bullet that hit it to its wave by that rounding then logs no hit (an instrumented GeneralPatton 1.54: 0 of 72). The list is ShieldTableData.x5Powers() (robots added by cards, the pc-tailor-field picks among them (BENCH-197, PR #686); ablation x5list from $X5_POWER, suffix x5).

Implemented in
Hadur, ShieldTableData, DuelController, PowerPolicy, ShieldList
Verified by
PowerPolicyTest.java, ShieldListTest.java
POW-17OptionalR9

Where the mode table's race-power: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, once at least 8 of our bullets and 8 of theirs have resolved this battle, the shot's power shall be the one of 0.1, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 1.95, 2.5 and 3.0 (below our energy) that maximises the expected score still to come in the round, ours less theirs, by the energy race: a value iteration over a grid of both robots' energies (finer below 20) in which each side fires at its gun-heat rate, a shot costs its power, a hit takes the bullet's damage and returns three times the power, the side left alive scores 80, and bullet damage scores as dealt; our hit chance is this battle's rate (with a 15-shot prior at 12%) moved to each power by the ratio of maximum escape angles, theirs is their rate (prior 10%) at the mean power they have fired, capped by their energy. It replaces the gun's choice, POW-5 and POW-1 to POW-4; SHIELD-4, END-3, RAM-1 and POW-10 still apply. A P record race-power shall say when the pick starts and stops. The list is ShieldTableData.racePowers() (the BENCH-206 picks, PR #695, that PR #698 banks; ablation rplist from $RACE_POWER, suffix rp or $RACE_POWER_SUFFIX).

Implemented in
Hadur, ShieldTableData, DuelController, EnergyRace, ShieldList
Verified by
EnergyRaceTest.java, ShieldListResourceTest.java
POW-18OptionalR9

Where the mode table's power-cap: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, the main gun's shot shall be at most 0.2 power (DuelController.CAP_POWER), and where its power-cap5: <name> lines name it, at most 0.5 (CAP_POWER_HALF), applied after every other power rule and before POW-10; shield mode's interception plans keep their own power, so a robot on the shield list is not given a power-cap line. tools/x5_card.py counts the lines as power-family levers (pk, p5). The lists are ShieldTableData.powerCaps() and powerCapHalves() (empty until a card holds robots; ablations pcaplist from $POWER_CAP, suffix pk, and pcap5list from $POWER_CAP5, suffix p5).

Implemented in
Hadur, ShieldTableData, DuelController, ShieldList
Verified by
ShieldListTest.java, ShieldListResourceTest.java
POW-19OptionalR9

Where the mode table's hard-fire: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, while our energy is 60 or more (DuelController.HARD_ENERGY), the main gun's shot shall be at least 0.6 power (HARD_POWER), never past the least power that kills (END-3), applied after POW-18 and before POW-10. It copies what BeepBoop does against these robots (the HARD class of diagnose_field.py: it fires hard while its energy is high and light once it is low). The list is ShieldTableData.hardFires() (Gilgalad since pc-locks round 10, PR #922, +7.1 on 12 fresh PC seeds; ablation hflist from $HARD_FIRE, suffix hf). A 1.95 floor against the HARD class's mid-field robots was screened on the cloud on 16 Nov and held on none, so it was removed.

Implemented in
Hadur, ShieldTableData, DuelController, ShieldList
Verified by
ShieldListTest.java
POW-20OptionalR9

Where the mode table's power-band: m20 m40 m60 m80 <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, while our energy is 20 or more, the main gun's shot shall be scaled by the multiplier for our energy band (20-40, 40-60, 60-80, 80 and up), held to 0.1-3.0, never raised past the least power that kills (END-3) and never above a POW-18 cap, applied after POW-19 and before POW-10. Each line copies BeepBoop's power-by-own-energy curve against that robot relative to ours, fitted from paired truth logs; the 0-20 band is left to the attrition work. The list is ShieldTableData.powerBands() (MoxieBot since pc-locks round 2, PR #845, +13.4 on 12 fresh PC seeds; ablation pblist from $POWER_BAND, suffix pb). A field screen of 77 fitted lines averaged about 0 on the cloud on 16 Nov, so it is a per-opponent lever only.

Implemented in
Hadur, ShieldTableData, DuelController, ShieldList
Verified by
ShieldListTest.java

Endgame

END-1StateS5

While enemy energy is below 16 and our energy exceeds 40 and the enemy gun heat exceeds ours, the distance policy shall set the target distance to 150 px.

Implemented in
Report, ShieldTableData, HadurCore, DuelController, EnergyLedger, ledger (package), RoundStats, SurfMover, move (package), core (package), DistancePolicy, Endgame, EnemyGunHeat, policy (package)
Verified by
DistancePolicyTest.java, EndgameTest.java, aggressive.feature
END-2StateS5

While enemy energy is 0, movement shall drive directly at the enemy.

Implemented in
Report, HadurCore, DuelController, RoundStats, SurfMover, move (package), core (package), Endgame, policy (package)
Verified by
EndgameTest.java, aggressive.feature
END-3EventR2

When the enemy's energy is at most a power-3 bullet's damage, the gun shall fire the least power that still kills on a hit, whatever power the gun or another rule chose.

Implemented in
DuelController, PowerPolicy
Verified by
PowerAffordabilityTest.java, PowerPolicyTest.java
END-4StateD2

While the enemy's energy is below the smallest bullet power it has fired this battle, the gun shall not fire a shot that would leave our energy less than 0.3 above the enemy's.

Implemented in
InactivityCheck, DuelController, PowerPolicy
Verified by
InactivityCheckTest.java, LastShotProperties.java, LastShotTest.java, druss.feature

Movement

MOVE-1UbiquitousS6

DEISE

DEISE-1UbiquitousE2

Each tick the bot shall drive toward the least-risk of 36 points on a ring of radius 0.45 of the target's distance, within 36 to 160 px, that keep 18 px off the walls (0.2 also kept its destination unless a ring point was 10% safer or the destination was within 16 px; off since 0.3).

Implemented in
Deise, DeiseLab, Move
Verified by
MoveTest.java
DEISE-2UbiquitousE3

The risk of a point shall include, for each live opponent, 106,667 plus 213,333 times its weight (DEISE-5's, set at every scan), over the squared distance to it (0.3: a flat 80,000; 0.2: 50,000 plus 500 times its energy).

Implemented in
Deise, Move
Verified by
MoveTest.java
DEISE-3StateE2

While no other robot is within 0.9 of the bot's distance from an opponent, the risk of a point shall include the absolute cosine between the heading to the point and the bearing from the point to that opponent (0.2 only; off since 0.3, Move.ACROSS).

Implemented in
Deise, DeiseLab, Move
Verified by
MoveTest.java
DEISE-4StateE2

While 4 or more opponents remain, the risk of a point shall include the number of opponents times its distance to the nearest corner over 5,000 (0.2 only; off since 0.3, Move.CORNERS).

Implemented in
Move
Verified by
MoveTest.java
DEISE-5EventE3

When an opponent is scanned, the bot shall set its weight to 1 if no other robot is within 0.9 of the bot's distance from it and 0.25 otherwise (since 0.4; 0.3 weighed only its shots). When its energy has dropped by 0.1 to 3 since its last scan, the bot shall record a wave, fired halfway back to that scan (at most 8 ticks), with a head-on and a linear line, weighed by that weight, in a ring of 64 slots; its danger at a point not yet passed is, for each line, the weight times 4,000 over 800 plus the squared distance from the point to that line's bullet at the point's range, added to the risk (in the shipped class since 0.3; 0.2 left the waves out).

Implemented in
Deise, DeiseLab, Move
Verified by
MoveTest.java
DEISE-6EventE0

When any opponent is scanned 1 to 8 ticks after its last scan, the bot shall record the transition from its last state to its present one, the turn spread over the gap, in that opponent's table, kept across rounds; a full row shall replace a random sample.

Implemented in
Deise, DeiseLab, Gun
Verified by
GunTest.java
DEISE-7StateE0

Implemented in

Verified by

DEISE-8StateE0

While it holds a sample, the gun shall play the target out by sampling its Markov chain, 400 playouts since 0.5 (200 in 0.3 and 0.4, 64 in 0.2), where a playout through a row of fewer than 8 samples votes at most 5 plus 12 for each sample of that row.

Implemented in
Gun
Verified by
GunTest.java
DEISE-9UbiquitousE0

A playout's vote shall cover the 1,024-bin circle over half the target's width at its distance, atan(9 / distance) either side since 0.5 (atan(18 / distance) to 0.4), and a playout that leaves the field shall vote 1.

Implemented in
Deise, Gun
Verified by
GunTest.java

DODGE

DODGE-1EventR9

When the duel makes a movement wave while the simple-aim dodge is on, the core shall record on it our position, heading, velocity, last turn and average velocity of the last 10 ticks and the enemy's position and heading, for the wave's tick and the two ticks before, and the wave shall give from them, per tick, where six simple aims send its bullet at its current power (head-on; linear, circular and averaged linear predicted tick by tick with paths stopped 18 px inside the walls; the one-line linear aim of bearing plus lateral velocity over bullet speed; and its arcsine), each aimed from the enemy's position on that tick, with our lateral velocity seen from the enemy and the enemy's body turn to the next tick.

Implemented in
DuelController, MoveDesign, Wave, ShotDodgePlanner, SimpleAims
Verified by
ShotDodgerTest.java
DODGE-2StateR9

While the simple-aim dodge is on for the opponent, the core shall count, across the battle's rounds, the enemy bullets whose heading it learns (a hit on us, a bullet meeting ours) and, per expert, those within 0.002 rad of it; the experts per tick being the six aims, the six with the enemy's body turn to the next tick added, and the one-line linear aim with a divisor learned from the bullets read (running mean decayed by 0.85 a bullet, samples between 4 and 30) with and without that turn. While one expert matches at least 3 of them and at least 60% of those read, the surf's danger for a wave shall be 1 when that expert's bullet falls inside the option's hit range (with a 0.005 rad margin) and 0 otherwise, plus a floor of 0.1 (so the distancing and escape factors still rank the safe options) and 0.05 of the views' score, without the hit-rate term; otherwise the views decide as before. At each round's end it shall write SD,round,tick,best,bestMatches,read,active, best being the expert's index (tick lag times 14 plus its place).

Implemented in
ShieldTableData, DuelController, MoveDesign, MoveController, ShotDodgePlanner, SurfMover, ShotDodger, ShieldList
Verified by
ShotDodgerTest.java
DODGE-3StateR9

While in a duel, the simple-aim dodge shall be on against an opponent the table's shot-dodge: lines name (matched as the shield list is), or against every opponent with the dodge switch on (off by default); in melee and team, and against any other opponent, it shall be off and the duel play exactly as before.

Implemented in
Hadur, ShieldTableData, DuelController, MoveDesign, ShieldList
Verified by
ShotDodgerTest.java
DODGE-4StateR9

While in a duel, the shot-dodge planner (DODGE-6) shall be on against an opponent the table's shot-dodge2: expert name lines name (matched as the shield list is), or against every opponent with the planner switch on (off by default); its expert (DODGE-2's index, or -1 for none) shall prime that expert's score with 3 bullets' worth, a line with a malformed or out-of-range expert shall be skipped, and in melee and team, and against any other opponent, the planner shall be off and the duel play exactly as before.

Implemented in
Hadur, ShieldTableData, DuelController, ShotDodger, ShieldList
Verified by
ShotDodgerTest.java
DODGE-5StateR9

While the planner is on, each enemy bullet read (DODGE-2) shall also score every expert exp(-d²/2σ²) for its distance d from the bullet's heading, σ being 0.18 of the wave's escape angle, and each firing wave that passes us neither hitting us nor meeting our bullet shall count, against every expert whose bullet lies inside the wave's precise intersection less 0.005 rad each side, one contradiction; the planner's expert shall be the one with the highest score plus prime less contradictions, and it shall act while that net is at least 3 and the score plus prime is at least 60% of the bullets read plus prime. At each round's end it shall write DP,round,tick,planBest,planScore,read,passes,contradictions,planActive,planTicks. DODGE-2's strict reading shall be unchanged.

Implemented in
DuelController, MoveController, ShotDodger
Verified by
ShotDodgerTest.java
DODGE-6StateR9

Where the mode table's ram-circ: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, while RAM-2's escape drives with RAM-5's dodge on, each enemy shot read shall also be kept with a third aim, circular: our heading turning at our last tick's turn rate at our speed then, until the bullet would reach us; that aim shall count against a candidate path as RAM-5's two do. The bench's ramcirc ablation forces it on every rammer; rclist names the robots in $RAM_CIRC. Rationale: the field's nano rammers (RammingC, MaxRisk and their kind) aim circularly, and the escape's wall-smoothed arcs are what that aim predicts; on 12 fresh cloud seeds at the live turn time it locked +6.3 ± 2.4 (nanoPri), +5.2 ± 1.7 (RammingC), +5.3 ± 1.6 (Galaxy03) points.

RAM-15OptionalR9

Where the mode table's ram-flee: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, while the enemy is within 200 px and its own speed toward us is 2 px/tick or more, the duel shall drive RAM-2's escape (with RAM-5's dodge) instead of the surf, whether or not RAM-2 has confirmed a rammer, until the enemy is more than 300 px away; the bullet power shall stay as RAM-1 sets it. RAM-17OptionalR9

Where the mode table's ram-fan: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, while RAM-2's escape drives with RAM-5's dodge on, each kept linear or circular aim shall be tested as three rays, the aim and 4 degrees either side, and a candidate path any ray would hit shall cost RAM-14's 120 px of closest approach. Rationale: against the field's point-blank rammers we flee at full speed for 70-90% of their shots and are hit 41-51% there, BeepBoop 13-16%; their guns are plain linear (or circular) aims, but they lag our state by a tick and turn at a limited rate, so the escape's single-line model put the bullet on us for only 49-57% of the real hits at the impact tick, and 67-76% as a +-4 degree fan (coverage/shards/pbcensus-D/slow). RAM-18OptionalR9

Where the mode table's ram-bend: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, for 10 ticks after each shot it fires, RAM-2's escape shall leave out every candidate whose first heading lies within 25 degrees of the direction we were travelling when it fired, choosing as before among the rest (and as before when none is left). Rationale: under 150 px a linear aim would hit 32% of our escape paths against BeepBoop's 10% (head-on equal at 15%); we run dead straight at full speed through 25% of the rammers' close shots, BeepBoop 3%, because the escape drives fixed absolute headings, the very path a linear aim predicts, and RAM-5/14/17 only weigh shots already in flight (coverage/shards/dzlane/L/rclass/close-diag.md). On the 12 rammers that play the escape arm it cut linear exposure to 18% and their close hit rate from 51% to 43%: pooled +1.85+-0.60 score points vs the base and +1.83+-0.65 vs the A/A twin on 12 fresh cloud seeds at the live turn time (79700), +1.7 on the 6-seed screen. Machete (-0.7) is left out. RAM-20OptionalR9

Where the mode table's ram-learn: <name> lines name an opponent (matched as the shield list is), in a duel against it, each enemy bullet whose heading the core learns (a hit on us, a bullet meeting ours) shall score 36 candidate aims, the six simple aims of DODGE-1 at the firing wave's tick and the two ticks before and each again with the enemy's body turn to the next tick added, by exp(-d²/2σ²) for its distance d from the bullet's heading, σ being 2 degrees; and while RAM-2's escape drives with RAM-5's dodge on, and the leading candidate's score is at least 2 and at least 0.2 of the bullets read, each enemy shot read shall be kept, in place of RAM-5's aims, as the three leading candidates known for its wave, each three rays (the aim and 3 degrees either side) weighted by its share of the three scores; a ray shall count against a candidate path on a tick when the stretch it flies that tick passes within 22 px, on both axes, of where the path stood before that tick's move (the engine's own order), and the path shall lose 240 px of closest approach times the aim's weight, half of it for the aim's own ray and a quarter for each ray beside it; with no line, before the bar is cleared, or for a wave none of whose known candidates clears it, the escape shall weigh shots as RAM-5, RAM-11, RAM-14 and RAM-17 state. At each round's end it shall write RL,round,tick,best,bestScore,read. Rationale: against 16 close-range robots with simple guns, 85% of the damage we take beyond a reference surfer's (DrussGT 3.1.20, 85.0 score share to our 81.0 over 64 traced battles) comes from shots a dodge existed for, and distance is not the cause (the reference takes more of its shots under 100 px, 50% to our 45%). RAM-5 predicts each bullet with two fixed aims, head-on and linear at one tick, while these guns fire about six aim variants across three ticks of lag: replayed offline, the fixed pair reproduces our real hit rates (45/20/17% at 60-100/100-150/150-250 px) and a mixture learned from the bullets that hit us gives 24/10/7%. In battle the model cut the hit rate in every band (60-100 px 49% to 41%, 100-150 px 26% to 16%, 150-250 px 17% to 9%) and damage taken by 13%. Cloud lock, 12 fresh seeds on the 16 robots with the model on for all: +1.40±0.52 pp against the A/A copy and +1.21±0.43 against 3.24 (A/A -0.19±0.48); Dreadnaught, NanoDeath, Bicephal, FollowFire and MaxRisk clear the bar alone against both controls, no robot's interval lies wholly below zero. On the other 54 robots that carry rammer lines, 12 fresh seeds: +0.80±0.16 against the A/A copy and +0.77±0.15 against 3.24 (A/A level), 18 robots clearing the bar alone and one, Kuramatron, wholly below zero (-2.5±1.3, -1.5±1.4), which is why it is a per-opponent line and not a default. A heavier cost alone (300, 600) changed nothing, an ungraded cost left the 60-100 px band unmoved (the nine rays left no clear path half the time), and braking or reversing paths gained nothing offline at twice the CPU. Cost: 6.5 skipped turns a battle against 3.2. Off by default; on 12 fresh PC seeds at the live turn time (pc-locks round 23, A/A -0.14±0.19) the model gained +1.98±0.44 pp on 25 robots, every one positive, and the 19 whose interval lies wholly above zero carry ram-learn lines in ramLearns(); pc-locks round 24 (PR #1024) added 17 more, 16 that hold it with a ram-known line (RAM-21) and Predator alone (+1.9), 36 in all.
